    dna = ennoia_505013

	learning = 10
	martial = 25
	prowess = 100
	trait = "vampire"
	trait = "gangrel"
	trait = "arrogant"		
	trait = "vengeful"
	trait = "callous"
    trait = "scarred"
	trait = "celerityadvanced"
	trait = "fortitudeadvanced"
	trait = "proteanadvanced"
	trait = "potenceadvanced"
	trait = "animalismadvanced"
	trait = "presenceadvanced"
	trait = "vampgeneration3"
	trait = "athletic"
	father = 505003
	female = yes
	give_nickname = nick_the_savage
	disallow_random_traits = yes
	
	40.1.1 = {
		birth = yes
		effect = {

			#add_animal_lifestyle_xp = 40000
			#add_melancholy_lifestyle_xp = 20000

			POD_give_permanent_injury_effect = { TRAIT = scarred }

			set_immortal_age = 31
		}
		effect = {
            add_character_flag = has_scripted_appearance
            add_character_flag = POD_portrait_female_hair_western_02_flag					
		}			
			add_trait = torpor
	}	
	
	69.1.1 = {	
		effect = { 
			set_relation_rival = { reason = rival_historical target = character:505006 }
			}
		effect = {
			add_trait = lifestyle_hunter
			add_trait_xp = {
                trait = lifestyle_hunter
				track = hunter
                value = 100
            }
		}			
	}

	71.1.1 = {
		death = {
			death_reason = death_disappearance 
		}
	}				
}
